No downstream runtime implementation/admission or independent execution is claimed.** Gate3 was accepted by the programme owner in this thread: “Yes, I accept. Come execute Lease 4.1.” The evident reference is Leaf4.1 of the accepted root plan. Acceptance permits this design investigation; it does not admit any source, profile, private carrier, module, migration or mathematical change. Main remains unchanged. P04/P05/P06/P09 have no downstream canonical output and are not re-created by an old M number. Historical95/30/107 mappings remain historical; no numeric M reservation or allocation occurs.

The effective original calculation brief/catalogue and accepted row overlays govern mathematics; accepted B/S/N/N6/D028 contracts govern meaning. ADR1/ADR2/Pass08 supply the foundation/private-local independence direction, reconciled by later accepted Gate2 quantities and the master, not competing plans. Relevant laws are AK-OWN/STATE, AK-DEP/SEAM, implementation ingress and lifecycle/proof law. Current M01 remains the registered canonical owner; proposed peer publication and reader/write boundaries require separately accepted ingress.

**Inspected evidence:** accepted G3 report sections2–15 and all83downstream actual-behaviour/path/test/evidence rows; original brief K07–K16 and §§4/6/8–17; effective direct graph and overlay-correct G3.3 raw closures; current canonical/context/score/occurrence carriers `api.py:89-94,280-332,336-514,571-672,674-798`, actual child scoping and history/entry paths `:1809-2174,2560-2872`; structured entry readers `_stage1.py:2882-2968`; current financial producer checks `_financial_policy.py:58-78` and G3 full repair chronology. The12older supplied archives were not needed for an identified new historical/authority question and were not mixed into the accepted current tree. No application imports, test/lint/type/runtime-validator/DB/provider/API execution occurs. Only document/source reading, standard-library parsing and Git/file integrity checks are used.

## 2. One local computation contract, instantiated independently per peer

### 2.1 Lawful ingress and calculation boundary

Each proposed peer's runtime graph may use (a) M00's admitted public raw observation/history projection, (b) exact future admitted foundation public occurrences/history and (c) its own immutable definition/model/context versions and own retained history. These are proposed edges: current M00 declares existing reader permissions and current M01 owns all D; future exact permits/public seams/writer roles must be admitted under WI-01/04, not inferred from a public-looking Python name. A composition caller may supply exact context identities/intent facts but no computed peer answer, and cannot hide a peer import or state read behind an adapter.

`F(Dxxx)` means a shared foundation public occurrence/history requirement, not a call to old M01 or a claim that the result is currently available. The DAG stops at that boundary; its foundation ancestors stay shared. Each local `Rxxx` binding uses M00 public access, exact source/mapping/unit/subject/selection/time/history versions. Full raw ancestry through F is dependency awareness, not permission to bypass F and recompute neutral mathematics locally. Book walking and policy/exposure/scenario calculations stay in the peer; neutral shared book measurements stay in the foundation; source-certified stream state/readiness/reset requirements remain mandatory for either path.

### 2.2 Local identity, definitions and immutable configuration

`C(Pxx,Dnnn)` is an output calculated and published by its one proposed canonical owner Pxx only after lawful admission. `pxx.local_nnn` is proposed private vocabulary for arithmetic *equivalent to the accepted Dnnn requirement*, with no canonical D publication or registry identity. It has its own definition revision, parameter/model versions, selected subject/candidate/path/stream scope and occurrence identity. The same private vocabulary in a different peer is a different owner-local object. Semantic D references remain provenance to accepted mathematics, never runtime routes to the peer associated with that D.

Every invocation fixes a content-resolvable **per-node**, not merely per-family, manifest: effective definition/catalogue row plus accepted overlay chain; local copy revision; exact parameter/schema/context/convention versions; source/selection policy; as-of and knowledge cutoff; candidate/order/live scope; history/forecast origin/path or book generation where applicable. A parent P_SCORE profile never supplies a child's unrelated feature schema. Mutable peer-owned configuration, shared strategy registries, peer caches/model services and private imports are prohibited. Equivalent downstream mathematics is copied, configured and proved locally; no shared P_SCORE/model/utility/risk service is introduced. Pure technical transport mechanisms do not acquire semantic ownership or excuse a peer dependency.

### 2.3 Execution, time and refusal

For each output request, walk its explicit local DAG below, obtain exact public F/R leaves and evaluate actual own producers in dependency order. Resolve node-scoped profiles and contexts before input selection; preserve type, units, contract/event/candidate/universe and version joins. History nodes select their required earlier origins/windows under their accepted laws; no arbitrary global latest or fallback scalar. Memoisation, if later justified, is per peer and keyed by the full logical scope and immutable inputs/versions/time, never another peer's canonical D cache.

A missing binding, undefined extractor, insufficient/wrongly timed history, duplicate/conflicting identity, incompatible unit/snapshot/model, unreconstructed/reset book or unresolved prerequisite refuses the affected result with a retained reason; it never fabricates zero or silently substitutes another model/source. Legitimate observed zero, known false/no_trade, censored/no-completed event and absent/unready input remain distinct according to each accepted row. The static DAG is a superset of required branches: D065 may lawfully omit future D062 in the accepted post-event branch, and side-conditional OI/input selection remains conjunctive only where applicable. No table invents a newly mandatory raw or D edge.

### 2.4 State, lineage, read and write boundaries

Each peer owns its local derivations, immutable manifest records, necessary entry/model/forecast/window/path/cursor history and its assigned canonical outputs. Only that peer's authorised writer may persist those records; readers receive its permitted public output/read contract without local strategy state access. No peer can read another peer's private tables/results/history, and no peer relies on another peer to calculate or persist an intermediate. A shared physical PostgreSQL deployment is possible only with separate semantic writer/reader roles; exact schema/API implementation is not selected or created here.

Private lineage uses tagged references identifying **raw M00 occurrence**, **shared foundation occurrence**, **same-owner canonical occurrence**, or **same-owner private derivation**. Each reference preserves actual owner/node/definition/profile/context/convention/input identities, value shape/units, as-of/cutoff and source provenance. Canonical publishing accepts only the assigned output set; a private record cannot be renamed Dnnn to pass a canonical serializer. New logical identities must distinguish owner/local node as well as selected inputs/version/time; current owner-absent M01 hashes cannot be copied blindly. Exact identity encoding, compatibility and roles are separately admitted before writes (WI-11/12/13/34).

Retries are deterministic/idempotent over identical logical facts/versions; changed sources/contexts/versions append new history, not overwrite old calculated truth. A parent references exactly the producer instances used, not embedded assertions alone. Retain real shape/context/convention/source snapshot/quantity/currency evidence, especially F4 risk carriers. Public durable readback and replay must recover the same actual local chain. M01 historical state/consumer inventory remains UNKNOWN; this design performs no state transfer or retirement.

### 2.5 Concrete independence proof to be obtained later

For each P section, instantiate only that peer, lawful M00/foundation public seams and its own admitted version/context/history stores. Omit every other peer package/service/schema/configuration. Exercise the actual canonical output path from source/context inputs, including full private producers; prefilled scalar utility/probability/state/risk carriers do not prove closure. Static owner/import checks, negative private-read permissions, per-peer type/public tests, durable lineage/retry/history proofs and relevant supported3.13.5/PostgreSQL evidence follow later authority. Change a different peer's local copy/model version and show this peer's existing result/manifest unaffected. Group-specific adversaries are listed below. This is a proof design, not executed evidence.

## 3. Explicit prerequisite boundaries that the static D frontier cannot settle

| ID / receiver | Established current fact and design choice | Exact unmet prerequisite / consequence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| IP-IDENTITY / G4I-001 / WI-34 | `DerivedSignalOccurrenceV1` requires canonical D identity; ScoreFeature/context and actual producer metadata are canonical-oriented. EntryStateContext resolves an exact persisted canonical D155, not an inline state. Proposed peers need separate private derivation records and actual local producer references. | Admit exact versioned private-reference/structured-component compatibility before production; preserve arithmetic and original entry identity evidence. For D186 choose an independently retained/reproduced local entry state from the original origin manifest and explicitly authorised association to the intended canonical anchor. A naked canonical ID without complete origin/provenance cannot be fabricated, treated as equal by a hash, relabelled or fetched from P12. The original anchor contract remains governing until proper compatibility/definition authority accepts the counterpart; affected old entry instances are BLOCKED, not silently converted. Finance/attribution consumers must validate actual local producer refs instead of weakening F4 evidence. |
| IP-DYNAMIC / G4I-002 / WI-35 | RequiredFieldPolicy, source coverage, revaluation conventions, training origins, attribution/evaluation paths, forecasts/outcomes and scheduling carry additional references beyond fixed D lists. D204/D205 have no static R/D edges but still have forecast/outcome prerequisites. | Freeze a finite per-instance expanded binding ledger: recursively classify each referenced value as lawful M00/public F, same-peer node/history, or required exact model/source/authority specification. Required-field absence requires an admitted projection; revaluation never chooses hidden curves/model; origin-causal training and later outcomes remain separate. EvaluationSet uses exact locked producer definitions/origin manifests and own retained/reproduced forecasts plus lawful observed outcomes, not peer-calculated answers smuggled into context or raw M00. If a locked model/forecast is outside the declared local repertoire, obtain its exact specification and design full local closure before admission; no invented universal model or new canonical allocation. Missing model/origin/outcome source remains BLOCKED. Likewise schedule grammar/cursor and path sources must be explicit. |
| IP-CANDIDATE / G4I-003 / WI-36 | CandidateSet contains identities/classes/neighbours, not D165/D173/D190 answers; the current generic child path does not establish full per-candidate fan-out. Existing G3I-009 independently records ignored eligibility/duplicate-carrier source discrepancies. | Resolve each candidate/neighbour to its exact local context and full local producer chain at one applicable cutoff/scenario/currency scope; distinguish node×candidate×origin instances. Reject duplicates/foreign labels. Preserve explicit order/ties/neighbour direction and resolve class applicability from authority, not invented string enums. Unknown class meaning needs a bounded model/authority decision before affected selection. No P18/P19/P25 result service or computed context payload supplies closure. |

All B4 holds remain OPEN for actual source/profile/model admission with G3 receivers. The fixed DAGs below terminate at public leaves or these named prerequisites; they do not pretend to know actual market mappings, coefficients, option pricing/exercise/carry choices, live store content or historical forecast origins. There is no new mathematical approval request for already accepted F4/row equations. Separate compatibility authority is required only where the future identity/public contract changes, not for rereading old proofs. G4.2 will sequence these propositions and implementation proofs; no extra gate per reference or peer receipt is created here.

## 4. Source-grounded semantic nodes (one card per downstream requirement)

Each card is instantiated in every relevant P DAG with that peer's own config/state/occurrence scope. Source locations are existing M01 candidates to extract or repair, not future imports; missing producers require new local implementation. G3's test/historical/acceptance fields retain their exact scope and are not repeated as blanket current acceptance.

## 6. Design closure, evidence limits and next work

The26fixed DAGs cover83unique canonical outputs and every external non-foundation requirement in the accepted Gate2 frontier. They supply concrete per-peer local identities/routes, context/version/lineage and state/read/write contracts, source-based reuse/repair/new actions and absent-peer proof scenarios. A separate canonical requirement card gives actual existing behaviour/source and completion action once per83requirements; each private instance resolves to it and its same-owner inputs. The design is more than an ancestor list: it specifies how to calculate/persist/replay each private dependency and exactly which context-induced paths remain blocked pending their missing specifications.

**Assessment result:** G4.1 is satisfied as a conditional documentary design: every fixed route terminates lawfully or at §3's named prerequisite, and no required current production/proof fact is asserted from the map. Instances needing IP-IDENTITY/IP-DYNAMIC/IP-CANDIDATE are not executable/admitted until their exact contracts, raw/profile/model versions and proof exist. Full local lineage/canonical anchor compatibility is a proposed design, not an automatically accepted amendment to the old M01 contracts. No static successful walk proves code isolation, numerical correctness, actual source supply, forecasts or database runtime behaviour.

**Verified by documentary checks:** all26accepted group output/frontier sets match independent graph reconstruction;83canonical outputs occur once;126foundation families and all prior G2/G3 fields remain unchanged except additive notes/new G4 fields; every local node's declared D inputs resolve to the same peer or F, graph order is acyclic/dependency-first and no foundation arithmetic is copied; all named prerequisites/issues/work references resolve. Context-induced paths are explicitly excluded from any fixed-graph completeness claim and received in WI-35. New38issue/228link expected populations are checked against actual IDs, including the preserved35/196G3checkpoint. The only repository changes are plan/report/register/backlog and this one map. No production/tests/schema/config/workbook/catalogue/registry/frozen governance/main changes. Fresh full-history archive checks include gzip EOF/CRC, extraction/hash equality, exact refs/commit, clean readback and Git fsck.

**Next:** G4.2 must turn this map and36-item initial backlog into the separate dependency-ordered implementation/acceptance roadmap covering all six master §8 phases and all54inherited/eightB4 obligations. That roadmap is NOT_STARTED in this pass. Gate4 remains IN_PROGRESS, not accepted. No implementation/IBKR acquisition or integration, module allocation, source/profile/model/runtime admission, live account/order change, historical migration or retirement occurs.
